- **document-typesetting**: Archived historical reference only; use the root SKILL.md for current document authoring. Original scope: write and typeset papers, whitepapers, reports and memos as print-quality PDFs in a restrained grayscale house style. Use when asked to produce a document meant to be read as a PDF or on paper. ([windborne/whitepaper-template](https://github.com/windborne/whitepaper-template/tree/HEAD/experiments/original))
- **document-ui**: Harmony の UI 各画面の操作リファレンス (docs/user-guide/ui-reference/) を、Playwright MCP screenshot + a11y snapshot を使って自動生成・更新する。引数なし = 全画面網羅。`&lt;screen-key&gt;` 指定で 1 画面のみ更新可能。発動条件: ユーザーが UI ドキュメント (操作マニュアル / 画面リファレンス / screenshot 付き使い方) の作成・更新を明示的に依頼した時のみ。spec / schema / code generation の説明依頼では起動しない。 ([csilost2001/harmony](https://github.com/csilost2001/harmony/tree/HEAD/ai-skills/document-ui))
- **document-ui-feature**: Maintain the owner's Dristi feature history at meaningful decision or implementation milestones, or when asked to document work. Record what changed and why; historical records are not routine planning or build inputs. ([pucardotorg/dristi-fe-develop](https://github.com/pucardotorg/dristi-fe-develop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/document-ui-feature) · ★8 · 3 copies)
- **document-understanding**: Read, probe, extract, summarize, or inspect user-uploaded PDF, Office, text, Markdown, CSV, HTML, OpenDocument, and similar document files. Use when a request references an uploaded document, attachment, its contents, metadata, file type, or parsing warnings. 适用于读取、解析、提取、总结或检查用户上传的文档、附件、表格及其内容。 ([yuanplussfive/senera](https://github.com/yuanplussfive/senera/tree/HEAD/System/Skills/document-understanding) · ★14)
- **document-understanding**: Reads fields and tables out of documents (invoices, receipts, purchase orders, statements, IDs, payslips, forms, any PDF or scan) with a Document Understanding reader, by conversation with a non-technical person: set up or reuse a reader, confirm its fields in plain words, try it on one sample, then build the flow with the Document Understanding nodes and a human check for uncertain documents. Use when the person wants values read from documents. ([robomotionio/agent-skills](https://github.com/robomotionio/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/document-understanding) · ★5 · 2 copies)
